Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the biotech space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 233 and the convergence rate is O(104/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to biotech structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 233/104 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
